# Care-instruction translation check, 2026-11-26

Scope: Admin API only (read, then body_html translations only). 328 active products, 223 with a care statement in the English description, 230 care statements x 20 locales = 4600 pairs aligned (care element found in the translated body for every pair; 0 (d)).
Method: automatic concept checks (machine/hand, cold, bleach+negation, tumble/line dry/flat, inside out, iron, steam, dry clean) per language, then a manual read of every string the checks flagged plus a read of the clause lists of the weakest locales; known bad phrases per locale were turned into rules. Judgment is non-native; strings that passed the automatic checks were only partly read (limitation).

## Totals by type (pairs; a pair can have more than one type)

- (a) polarity/meaning: 1
- (b) temperature/number: 5
- (c) nonsense or mistranslated key words / mangled label: 563
- (d) care statement missing: 0
- (e) label left in English: 164
- other: care element merged with unrelated content or leaked internal text (listed, not edited): 41

## Outcome

- FIXED: 573
- LISTED: 78

Fix method: whole care statement replaced by a composition from `care-phrasebook.json` (one standard phrase per instruction per language); label-only findings: only the label word replaced. Before-state per product in `<handle>-before.json`. Not fixed: care element merged with other content (cannot isolate), English statement outside the standard phrase set, or no phrasebook for the locale; these stay in the list below for follow-up.

## Addendum (V33 round)
- Second pass (apply2): 75 of the 78 listed pairs rewritten; ja 26 are not defects (unclosed `<li>` only, care text correct); 3 "Soft Summer" duplicates were already fixed.
- Phrasebook now 20 locales x 59 keys incl. V33-3 corrections (`care-phrasebook.json`); closing rule: every care element = composition from the book, checked in code after each write (`notcomposed 0`).
- Closing rewrite (`apply3.py`, log `apply3-log.txt`) runs sequentially, one product at a time; slow because each translationsRegister call is slow. Parallel workers were refused by the permission classifier and not retried.
- Excluded (not a care section / embedded in longer text): "Carefree Maintenance", 4 "Easy-care fabric, machine washable" lines, "Soft Summer Build", and the two elements with Occasions/Sizing text before "Care:".
